IBR_dissolving_the_union = {
	IBR_dissolve_union = {
		available = {
			has_country_flag = IBR_divorce_decision_flag
		}

		allowed = {
			tag = IBR
		}

		visible = {
			has_country_flag = IBR_divorce_decision_flag
		}

		fire_only_once = yes

		cost = 0

		ai_will_do = { #KD's Notes - AI divorces through an event
			factor = 0
		}

		days_remove = 1

		complete_effect = {
			log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: Decision IBR_dissolve_union"
			custom_effect_tooltip = IBR_divorce_game_over_tt
			if = {
				limit = { has_country_flag = IBR_franco_dead }
				country_event = {
					id = IBR_divorce.3
					hours = 6
				}
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { has_country_flag = IBR_Salazar_dead }
				country_event = {
					id = IBR_divorce.2
					hours = 6
				}
			}
			else = {
				country_event = {
					id = IBR_divorce.1
					hours = 6
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

				econ_gdp_growth_change = yes
			}

				limit = {

					set_temp_variable = { temp_econ_GDP_boost_percentage = 0.02 }

				set_temp_variable = { gdp_growth_temp = 1 }
				econ_gdp_growth_change = yes

				limit = {
					COL = { check_variable = { ECON_SPHERE_LEADER = IBR } }
					COL = { check_variable = { ECON_SPHERE_LEADER = GER } }

					set_temp_variable = { gdp_growth_temp = 0.7 }
					econ_gdp_growth_change = yes
				}

					CLC = { check_variable = { ECON_SPHERE_LEADER = GER } }

					set_temp_variable = { gdp_growth_temp = 0.7 }
					econ_gdp_growth_change = yes
				}
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					CLD = { check_variable = { ECON_SPHERE_LEADER = IBR } }
					CLD = { check_variable = { ECON_SPHERE_LEADER = GER } }
					country_exists = CLD
				}
				CLD = {
					set_temp_variable = { temp_econ_GDP_boost_percentage = 0.02 }

													building = synthetic_refinery
													size > 1
													include_locked = yes
												}
												OR = {

									}

	IBR_invite_Siemens_decision = {
		icon = GFX_decision_generic_money
		allowed = {
			tag = IBR
		}
		visible = {
			has_country_flag = IBR_pakt_invite_corporations
		}
		fire_only_once = no
		fixed_random_seed = no
		cost = 70
		ai_will_do = {
			factor = 1
		}
		days_remove = 30
		days_re_enable = 100

		remove_effect = {
			log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: Decision remove IBR_invite_Siemens_decision"
			random_list = {
				20 = {
					set_temp_variable = { gdp_growth_temp = 0.5 }
					econ_gdp_growth_change = yes
				}
				40 = {
					IBR = {
						add_tech_bonus = {
							name = electronics
							bonus = 0.5
							uses = 1
							category = electronics
						}
					}
				}
				40 = {
					IBR = {
						random_owned_controlled_state = {
							limit = {
								# Can build some more
								free_building_slots = {
									building = radar_station
									size > 1
									include_locked = no
								}
								# Only need to be border/coastal/island
								OR = {
									is_coastal = yes
									any_neighbor_state = {
										NOT = {
											is_controlled_by = IBR
										}
									}
								}
								# Don't overcrowd
								OR = {
									NOT = {
										# Returns false if this province has no neighbor state
										any_neighbor_state = {
											always = yes
										}
									}
									any_neighbor_state = {
										is_fully_controlled_by = IBR
										radar_station < 1
									}
								}
								# Try to upgrade
								OR = {
									radar_station > 0
									NOT = {
										owner = {
											any_owned_state = {
												# Can build some more
												free_building_slots = {
													building = radar_station
													size > 1
													include_locked = no
												}
												# Only need to be border/coastal/island
												OR = {
													is_coastal = yes
													any_neighbor_state = {
														NOT = {
															is_controlled_by = IBR
														}
													}
												}
												# Don't overcrowd
												OR = {
													NOT = {
														# Returns false if this province has no neighbor state
														any_neighbor_state = {
															always = yes
														}
													}
													any_neighbor_state = {
														is_fully_controlled_by = IBR
														radar_station < 1
													}
												}
												radar_station > 0
											}
										}
									}
								}
							}
							add_building_construction = {
								type = radar_station
								level = 1
								instant_build = yes
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}

		}

		icon = GFX_decision_generic_timer

		days_mission_timeout = 7
		complete_effect = {

		}
		timeout_effect = {
			log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: Decision timeout IBR_pre_election_check_mission"
			hidden_effect = {
				activate_mission = IBR_pre_election_check_mission
				set_temp_variable_to_random = {
					var = IBR_pre_election_influence_change_temp
					min = IBR_pre_election_opposition_min_gain
					max = IBR_pre_election_opposition_max_gain
				}
				set_temp_variable = { IBR_pre_election_category_temp = 1 }
				IBR_pre_election_change_influence = yes
				set_temp_variable_to_random = {
					var = IBR_pre_election_influence_change_temp
					min = IBR_pre_election_conservatives_min_gain
					max = IBR_pre_election_conservatives_max_gain
				}
				set_temp_variable = { IBR_pre_election_category_temp = 2 }
				IBR_pre_election_change_influence = yes
			}
			custom_effect_tooltip = IBR_pre_election_check_mission_tt
		}
	}

IBR_terrorism_category = {
	IBR_AAS_Funding_decision = {
		icon = GFX_decision_generic_money
		fire_only_once = no
		days_remove = 90
		allowed = {
			tag = IBR
		}
		available = { 
			OR = {
				has_political_power > 35
				NOT = { has_idea = civilian_budget_cuts }
			} 
		}
		visible = {
		}
		custom_cost_trigger = { always = yes }
		ai_will_do = {
			factor = 100
		}

		complete_effect = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_idea = civilian_budget_cuts
				}
				custom_effect_tooltip = IBR_AAS_decision_budget_cuts
				add_political_power = -35				
			}
		}
		remove_effect = {
			log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: Decision remove IBR_AAS_Funding_decision"
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_political_power > 100
				}
				add_political_power = -100

				set_temp_variable = { temp_econ_spending_amount = 0.03 }
				econ_spend_money_once_effect_raw_money = yes
				if = {
					limit = {
						OR = {
							AND = {
								has_government = despotism
								has_country_flag = IBR_advisory_council_created_flag
							}
							has_government = fascism
						}
					}

					set_temp_variable = { IBR_AAS_resources_temp = 5 }
					IBR_AAS_resources_change = yes
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						OR = {
							has_government = paternalism
							AND = {
								has_government = despotism
								has_country_flag = IBR_legislative_council_created_flag
							}
						}
					}

					set_temp_variable = { IBR_AAS_resources_temp = 7 }
					IBR_AAS_resources_change = yes
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_government = conservatism
					}

					set_temp_variable = { IBR_AAS_resources_temp = 9 }
					IBR_AAS_resources_change = yes
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						OR = {
							has_government = liberalism
							has_government = progressivism
						}
					}

					set_temp_variable = { IBR_AAS_resources_temp = 12 }
					IBR_AAS_resources_change = yes
				}
			}
			else = {
				custom_effect_tooltip = IBR_AAS_Funding_decision_low_pp_tt
				set_temp_variable = { temp_econ_spending_amount = 0.085 }
				econ_spend_money_once_effect_raw_money = yes

				if = {
					limit = {
						OR = {
							AND = {
								has_government = despotism
								has_country_flag = IBR_advisory_council_created_flag
							}
							has_government = fascism
						}
					}
					set_temp_variable = { IBR_AAS_resources_temp = 3 }
					IBR_AAS_resources_change = yes
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						OR = {
							has_government = paternalism
							AND = {
								has_government = despotism
								has_country_flag = IBR_legislative_council_created_flag
							}
						}
					}
					set_temp_variable = { IBR_AAS_resources_temp = 4 }
					IBR_AAS_resources_change = yes
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_government = conservatism
					}
					set_temp_variable = { IBR_AAS_resources_temp = 5 }
					IBR_AAS_resources_change = yes
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						OR = {
							has_government = liberalism
							has_government = progressivism
						}
					}
					set_temp_variable = { IBR_AAS_resources_temp = 7 }
					IBR_AAS_resources_change = yes
				}
			}			
		}
	}

	IBR_AAS_Military_decision = {
		icon = GFX_decision_generic_police_redeployment

			}
			add_manpower = -3000
			

			if = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						AND = {
							has_government = despotism
							has_country_flag = IBR_advisory_council_created_flag
						}
						has_government = fascism
					}
				}
				set_temp_variable = { IBR_AAS_resources_temp = 7 }
				IBR_AAS_resources_change = yes
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						has_government = paternalism
						AND = {
							has_government = despotism
							has_country_flag = IBR_legislative_council_created_flag
						}
					}
				}
				set_temp_variable = { IBR_AAS_resources_temp = 9 }
				IBR_AAS_resources_change = yes
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_government = conservatism
				}
				set_temp_variable = { IBR_AAS_resources_temp = 12 }
				IBR_AAS_resources_change = yes
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						has_government = liberalism
						has_government = progressivism
					}
				}
				set_temp_variable = { IBR_AAS_resources_temp = 16 }
				IBR_AAS_resources_change = yes
			}
		}

				is_subject_of = IBR
			}
		}

			}
		}

		days_mission_timeout = 180

		}

			log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: Decision timeout IBR_yves_war_mission"
			IBR = {
				white_peace = SLG
			}
			MOR = {
				white_peace = SLG
			}
			SLG = {
				transfer_state = 1154
				transfer_state = 1151
				transfer_state = 1147
				transfer_state = 785
				transfer_state = 514
				transfer_state = 460
				transfer_state = 1161
				transfer_state = 1149

				transfer_state = 1146
				transfer_state = 1145
				transfer_state = 1144
				transfer_state = 1143
				transfer_state = 1006
				transfer_state = 459
				transfer_state = 1007
				transfer_state = 513
				every_owned_state = {
					add_core_of = SLG
					remove_core_of = IBR
					remove_claim_by = IBR
				}
			}
			SLG = { country_event = { id = ALG_skeleton.8 } }
		}

		ai_will_do = {
			factor = 5
		}
	}

	IBR_DP_toggle_colonial_decisions = {
		allowed = { original_tag = IBR }
		icon = {
			key = GFX_decision_generic_visibility_closed

				has_country_flag = IBR_DP_show_colonial
			}
		}
		icon = {
			key = GFX_decision_generic_visibility
			trigger = {
				NOT = { has_country_flag = IBR_DP_show_colonial }
			}
		}
		visible = {
			has_country_flag = IBR_DP_started
			is_ai = no
		}

		complete_effect = {
			log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: Decision Complete IBR_DP_toggle_colonial_decisions"
			if = {
				limit = { has_country_flag = IBR_DP_show_colonial }
				custom_effect_tooltip = IBR_DP_colonial_decisions_1_tt
				clr_country_flag = IBR_DP_show_colonial
			}
			else = {
				custom_effect_tooltip = IBR_DP_colonial_decisions_2_tt
				set_country_flag = IBR_DP_show_colonial
			}
		}
	}

	IBR_DP_improve_infra_col = {
		allowed = { original_tag = IBR }
		icon = GFX_decision_generic_infrastructure
		visible = {
			OR = {
				has_country_flag = IBR_DP_show_colonial
				is_ai = yes
			}
		}

		cost = 20
		days_remove = 30
		fixed_random_seed = no

		complete_effect = {
			log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: Decision IBR_DP_improve_infra_col"

			set_temp_variable = { temp_econ_spending_amount = 0.02 }
			econ_spend_money_once_effect_raw_money = yes
		}
		remove_effect = {
			random_owned_controlled_state = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						state = 702
						state = 296
						state = 1548
						state = 1005
						state = 290
					}
				}
				if = {
					limit = { state = 702 }
					set_temp_variable = { IBR_state_selection = 1 }
					set_temp_variable = { state_value_multiplier_temp = 1.035 }
					econ_state_value_change_multiply = yes
				}
				else_if = {
					limit = { state = 296 }
					set_temp_variable = { IBR_state_selection = 2 }
					set_temp_variable = { state_value_multiplier_temp = 1.035 }
					econ_state_value_change_multiply = yes
				}
				else_if = {
					limit = { state = 1548 }
					set_temp_variable = { IBR_state_selection = 3 }
					set_temp_variable = { state_value_multiplier_temp = 1.035 }
					econ_state_value_change_multiply = yes
				}
				else_if = {
					limit = { state = 1005 }
					set_temp_variable = { IBR_state_selection = 4 }
					set_temp_variable = { state_value_multiplier_temp = 1.035 }
					econ_state_value_change_multiply = yes
				}
				else_if = {
					limit = { state = 290 }
					set_temp_variable = { IBR_state_selection = 5 }
					set_temp_variable = { state_value_multiplier_temp = 1.02 }
					econ_state_value_change_multiply = yes
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { IBR_state_selection = 1 } }
				set_temp_variable = { IBR_colonial_cabo_verde_revenue_mult = 0.04 }
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { IBR_state_selection = 2 } }
				set_temp_variable = { IBR_colonial_guinea_bissau_revenue_mult = 0.04 }
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { IBR_state_selection = 3 } }
				set_temp_variable = { IBR_colonial_equatorial_guinea_revenue_mult = 0.04 }
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { IBR_state_selection = 4 } }
				set_temp_variable = { IBR_colonial_western_sahara_revenue_mult = 0.03 } #Rif are moved to it if owned
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { IBR_state_selection = 5 } }
				set_temp_variable = { IBR_colonial_western_sahara_revenue_mult = 0.03 }
			}
			IBR_colonial_misc_revenue_change = yes
		}

	IBR_democratic_council_deliberation_mission = {
		allowed = { original_tag = IBR }
		icon = GFX_decision_generic_clock
		priority = 20

		activation = {
			always = no
		}
		available = {
			hidden_trigger = {
				always = no
			}
		}
		selectable_mission = no
		days_mission_timeout = 5
		timeout_effect = {
			log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: Decision timeout IBR_democratic_council_deliberation_mission"
			if = { #Law passes by absolute majority, law + reward
				limit = {
					check_variable = { IBR_council_total_supporting_seats > 299 }
				}
				hidden_effect = {
					country_event = {
						id = IBR_council_law.5
					}
				}
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { #Law passes by simple majority, only law applied
					check_variable = { IBR_council_total_supporting_seats > IBR_council_total_against_seats }
				}
				hidden_effect = {
					country_event = {
						id = IBR_council_law.6
					}
				}
			}
			else = { #Law fails, law doesn't pass + punishment
				hidden_effect = {
					country_event = {
						id = IBR_council_law.7
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}

IBR_caetano_springboard_category = {
	IBR_caetano_springboard_category_mission = {
		allowed = { original_tag = IBR }
		icon = GFX_decision_generic_clock
		priority = 20

		activation = {
			always = no
		}
		available = {
			hidden_trigger = {
				always = no
			}
		}
		selectable_mission = no
		days_mission_timeout = 28
		timeout_effect = {
			log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: Decision timeout IBR_caetano_springboard_category_mission"
			hidden_effect = {
				activate_mission = IBR_caetano_springboard_category_mission
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = { IBR_caudillo_popularity_bar > 74.99 }
				}
				set_temp_variable = { IBR_choupana_influence_temp = -2 }
				IBR_choupana_influence_change = yes
				set_temp_variable = { IBR_caetano_influence_temp = -2 }
				IBR_caetano_influence_change = yes
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = { IBR_caudillo_popularity_bar > 49.99 }
				}
				set_temp_variable = { IBR_choupana_influence_temp = -1 }
				IBR_choupana_influence_change = yes
				set_temp_variable = { IBR_caetano_influence_temp = -1 }
				IBR_caetano_influence_change = yes
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = { IBR_caudillo_popularity_bar > 24.99 }
				}
				set_temp_variable = { IBR_choupana_influence_temp = -0.5 }
				IBR_choupana_influence_change = yes
				set_temp_variable = { IBR_caetano_influence_temp = -0.5 }
				IBR_caetano_influence_change = yes
			}
			else = {
				set_temp_variable = { IBR_choupana_influence_temp = -0.25 }
				IBR_choupana_influence_change = yes
				set_temp_variable = { IBR_caetano_influence_temp = -0.25 }
				IBR_caetano_influence_change = yes
			}
		}
	}

	IBR_caetano_springboard_small_caetano_boost = {
